Rowe Price (2.013%) 2025 130,497 72,880 36,000","JPMorgan Chase (1.417%)","BlackRock Life (1.409%)","Eaton Vance (1.337%)","","GPU Technology Conference","Nvidia\u0026#39;s GPU Technology Conference (GTC) is a series of technical conferences held around the","world.[164] It originated in 2009 in San Jose, California, with an initial focus on the potential for solving","computing challenges through GPUs.[165] In recent years, the conference\u0026#39;s focus has shifted to various","applications of artificial intelligence and deep learning; including self-driving cars, healthcare, high-","performance computing, and Nvidia Deep Learning Institute (DLI) training.[166] GTC 2018 attracted over","8400 attendees.[164] GTC 2020 was converted to a digital event and drew roughly 59,000 registrants.[167]","","After several years of remote-only events, GTC in March 2024 returned to an in-person format in San","Jose, California.[168]","","Product families","Nvidia\u0026#39;s product families include graphics processing units, wireless communication devices, and","automotive hardware and software, such as:","","GeForce, consumer-oriented graphics processing products","","","","RTX, professional visual computing graphics","processing products (replacing GTX and Quadro)","NVS, a multi-display business graphics processor","Tegra, a system on a chip series for mobile devices","Tesla, line of dedicated general-purpose GPUs for","high-end image generation applications in","professional and scientific fields","nForce, a motherboard chipset created by Nvidia for","Intel (Celeron, Pentium and Core 2) and AMD","(Athlon and Duron) microprocessors A Shield Tablet with its accompanying","GRID, a set of hardware and services by Nvidia for input pen (left) and gamepad","graphics virtualization","Shield, a range of gaming hardware including the","Shield Portable, Shield Tablet and Shield TV","Drive, a range of hardware and software products for designers and manufacturers of","autonomous vehicles. The Drive PX-series is a high-performance computer platform aimed","at autonomous driving through deep learning,[169] while Driveworks is an operating system","for driverless cars.[170]","","BlueField, a range of data processing units, initially inherited from their acquisition of","Mellanox Technologies[171][172]","","Datacenter/server class CPU, codenamed Grace, released in 2023[173][174]","","DGX, an enterprise platform designed for deep learning applications","Maxine, a platform providing developers a suite of AI-based conferencing software[175]","","Open-source software support","Until September 23, 2013, Nvidia had not published any documentation for its advanced hardware,[176]","","meaning that programmers could not write free and open-source device drivers for its products without","resorting to reverse engineering.","","Instead, Nvidia provides its own binary GeForce graphics drivers for X.Org and an open-source library","that interfaces with the Linux, FreeBSD or Solaris kernels and the proprietary graphics software. Nvidia","also provided but stopped supporting an obfuscated open-source driver that only supports two-","dimensional hardware acceleration and ships with the X.Org distribution.[177]","","The proprietary nature of Nvidia\u0026#39;s drivers has generated dissatisfaction within free-software communities.","In a 2012 talk, Linus Torvalds gave a middle-finger gesture and criticized Nvidia’s stance toward","Linux.\u0026quot;[178][179] Some Linux and BSD users insist on using only open-source drivers and regard Nvidia\u0026#39;s","insistence on providing nothing more than a binary-only driver as inadequate, given that competing","manufacturers such as Intel offer support and documentation for open-source developers, and others like","AMD release partial documentation and provide some active development.[180][181]","","Nvidia only provides x86/x64 and ARMv7-A versions of their proprietary driver; as a result, features like","CUDA are unavailable on other platforms.[182] Some users claim that Nvidia\u0026#39;s Linux drivers impose","artificial restrictions, like limiting the number of monitors that can be used at the same time, but the","company has not commented on these accusations.[183]","","","","In 2014, with its Maxwell GPUs, Nvidia started to require firmware by them to unlock all features of its","graphics cards.[184][185][186]","","On May 12, 2022, Nvidia announced that they are opensourcing their GPU kernel modules.[187][188][189]","","Support for Nvidia\u0026#39;s firmware was implemented in nouveau in 2023, which allows proper power","management and GPU reclocking for Turing and newer graphics card generations.[190][191]","","In 21 July 2025, Nvidia announce to extend CUDA support to RISC-V.[192][193][194]","","List of Nvidia open-source projects","Nouveau","NVDLA","PhysX","VDPAU","Vibrante","GR00T[195]","","Deep learning","Nvidia GPUs are used in deep learning, and accelerated analytics due to Nvidia\u0026#39;s CUDA software","platform and API which allows programmers to utilize the higher number of cores present in GPUs to","parallelize BLAS operations which are extensively used in machine learning algorithms.[11] They were","included in many Tesla, Inc. vehicles before Musk announced at Tesla Autonomy Day in 2019 that the","company developed its own SoC and full self-driving computer now and would stop using Nvidia","hardware for their vehicles.[196][197] These GPUs are used by researchers, laboratories, tech companies","and enterprise companies.[198] In 2009, Nvidia was involved in what was called the \u0026quot;big bang\u0026quot; of deep","learning, \u0026quot;as deep-learning neural networks were combined with Nvidia graphics processing units","(GPUs)\u0026quot;.[199] That year, the Google Brain team used Nvidia GPUs to create deep neural networks","capable of machine learning, where Andrew Ng determined that GPUs could increase the speed of deep","learning systems by about 100 times.[200]","","DGX","DGX is a line of supercomputers by Nvidia.","","In April 2016, Nvidia produced the DGX-1 based on an 8 GPU cluster, to improve the ability of users to","use deep learning by combining GPUs with integrated deep learning software.[201] Nvidia gifted its first","DGX-1 to OpenAI in August 2016 to help it train larger and more complex AI models with the capability","of reducing processing time from six days to two hours.[202][203] It also developed Nvidia Tesla K80 and","P100 GPU-based virtual machines, which are available through Google Cloud, which Google installed in","November 2016.[204] Microsoft added GPU servers in a preview offering of its N series based on Nvidia\u0026#39;s","Tesla K80s, each containing 4992 processing cores. Later that year, AWS\u0026#39;s P2 instance was produced","using up to 16 Nvidia Tesla K80 GPUs. That month Nvidia also partnered with IBM to create a software","kit that boosts the AI capabilities of Watson,[205] called IBM PowerAI.[206][207] Nvidia also offers its own","","","","Nvidia Deep Learning software development kit.[208] In 2017, the GPUs were also brought online at the","Riken Center for Advanced Intelligence Project for Fujitsu.[209] The company\u0026#39;s deep learning technology","led to a boost in its 2017 earnings.[210]","","In 2018, Nvidia researchers demonstrated imitation-learning techniques for industrial robots. They have","created a system that, after a short revision and testing, can already be used to control the universal robots","of the next generation. In addition to GPU manufacturing, Nvidia provides parallel processing","capabilities to researchers and scientists that allow them to efficiently run high-performance","applications.[211]","","Robotics","In 2020, Nvidia unveiled \u0026quot;Omniverse\u0026quot;, a virtual environment designed for engineers.[212] Nvidia also","open-sourced Isaac Sim, which makes use of this Omniverse to train robots through simulations that","mimic the physics of the robots and the real world.[213][214]","","In 2024, Huang oriented Nvidia\u0026#39;s focus towards humanoid robots and self-driving cars, which he expects","to gain widespread adoption.[215][216]","","In 2025, Nvidia announced Isaac GR00T N1, an open-source foundation model \u0026quot;designed to expedite the","development and capabilities of humanoid robots\u0026quot;. Neura Robotics, 1X Technologies and Vention are","among the first companies to use the model.[217][218][219]","","Inception Program","Nvidia\u0026#39;s Inception Program was created to support startups making exceptional advances in the fields of","artificial intelligence and data science. Award winners are announced at Nvidia\u0026#39;s GTC Conference. In","May 2017, the program had 1,300 companies.[220] As of March 2018, there were 2,800 startups in the","Inception Program.[221] As of August 2021, the program has over 8,500 members in 90 countries, with","cumulative funding of US$60 billion.[222]","","Controversies","","Maxwell advertising dispute","","GTX 970 hardware specifications","Issues with the GeForce GTX 970\u0026#39;s specifications were first brought up by users when they found out","that the cards, while featuring 4  GB of memory, rarely accessed memory over the 3.5  GB boundary.","Further testing and investigation eventually led to Nvidia issuing a statement that the card\u0026#39;s initially","announced specifications had been altered without notice before the card was made commercially","available, and that the card took a performance hit once memory over the 3.5  GB limit were put into","use.[223][224][225]","","","","The card\u0026#39;s back-end hardware specifications, initially announced as being identical to those of the","GeForce GTX 980, differed in the amount of L2 cache (1.75 MB versus 2 MB in the GeForce GTX 980)","and the number of ROPs (56 versus 64 in the 980). Additionally, it was revealed that the card was","designed to access its memory as a 3.5 GB section, plus a 0.5 GB one, access to the latter being 7 times","slower than the first one.[226] The company then went on to promise a specific driver modification to","alleviate the performance issues produced by the cutbacks suffered by the card.[227] However, Nvidia","later clarified that the promise had been a miscommunication and there would be no specific driver","update for the GTX 970.[228] Nvidia claimed that it would assist customers who wanted refunds in","obtaining them.[229] On February 26, 2015, Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang went on record in Nvidia\u0026#39;s official","blog to apologize for the incident.[230] In February 2015 a class-action lawsuit alleging false advertising","was filed against Nvidia and Gigabyte Technology in the U.S. District Court for Northern","California.[231][232]","","Nvidia revealed that it is able to disable individual units, each containing 256  KB of L2 cache and 8","ROPs, without disabling whole memory controllers.[233] This comes at the cost of dividing the memory","bus into high speed and low speed segments that cannot be accessed at the same time unless one segment","is reading while the other segment is writing because the L2/ROP unit managing both of the GDDR5","controllers shares the read return channel and the write data bus between the two GDDR5 controllers and","itself.[233] This is used in the GeForce GTX 970, which therefore can be described as having 3.5 GB in its","high speed segment on a 224-bit bus and 0.5 GB in a low speed segment on a 32-bit bus.[233]","","On July 27, 2016, Nvidia agreed to a preliminary settlement of the U.S. class action lawsuit,[231] offering","a $30 refund on GTX 970 purchases. The agreed upon refund represents the portion of the cost of the","storage and performance capabilities the consumers assumed they were obtaining when they purchased","the card.[234]","","GeForce Partner Program","The Nvidia GeForce Partner Program was a marketing program designed to provide partnering","companies with benefits such as public relations support, video game bundling, and marketing","development funds.[235] The program proved to be controversial, with complaints about it possibly being","an anti-competitive practice.[236]","","First announced in a blog post on March 1, 2018,[237] it was canceled on May 4, 2018.[238]","","Hardware Unboxed","On December 10, 2020, Nvidia told YouTube tech reviewer Steven Walton of Hardware Unboxed that it","would no longer supply him with GeForce Founders Edition graphics card review units.[239][240] In a","Twitter message, Hardware Unboxed said, \u0026quot;Nvidia have officially decided to ban us from receiving","GeForce Founders Edition GPU review samples. Their reasoning is that we are focusing on rasterization","instead of ray tracing. They have said they will revisit this \u0026#39;should your editorial direction change.\u0026#39; \u0026quot;[241]","","In emails that were disclosed by Walton from Nvidia Senior PR Manager Bryan Del Rizzo, Nvidia had","said:","","","","...your GPU reviews and recommendations have continued to focus singularly on","rasterization performance, and you have largely discounted all of the other technologies we","offer gamers. It is very clear from your community commentary that you do not see things","the same way that we, gamers, and the rest of the industry do.[242]","","TechSpot, partner site of Hardware Unboxed, said, \u0026quot;this and other related incidents raise serious","questions around journalistic independence and what they are expecting of reviewers when they are sent","products for an unbiased opinion.\u0026quot;[242]","","A number of technology reviewers came out strongly against Nvidia\u0026#39;s move.[243][244] Linus Sebastian, of","Linus Tech Tips, titled the episode of his weekly WAN Show, \u0026quot;NVIDIA might ACTUALLY be","EVIL...\u0026quot;[245] and was highly critical of the company\u0026#39;s move to dictate specific outcomes of technology","reviews.[246] The review site Gamers Nexus said it was, \u0026quot;Nvidia\u0026#39;s latest decision to shoot both its feet:","They\u0026#39;ve now made it so that any reviewers covering RT will become subject to scrutiny from untrusting","viewers who will suspect subversion by the company. Shortsighted self-own from NVIDIA.\u0026quot;[247]","","Two days later, Nvidia reversed their stance.[248][249] Hardware Unboxed sent out a Twitter message, \u0026quot;I","just received an email from Nvidia apologizing for the previous email \u0026amp; they\u0026#39;ve now walked everything","back.\u0026quot;[250][243] On December 14, Hardware Unboxed released a video explaining the controversy from","their viewpoint.[251] Via Twitter, they also shared a second apology sent by Nvidia\u0026#39;s Del Rizzo that said","\u0026quot;to withhold samples because I didn\u0026#39;t agree with your commentary is simply inexcusable and crossed the","line.\u0026quot;[252][253]","","Improper disclosures about cryptomining","In 2018, Nvidia\u0026#39;s chips became popular for cryptomining, the process of obtaining crypto rewards in","exchange for verifying transactions on distributed ledgers, the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission","(SEC) said. However, the company failed to disclose that it was a \u0026quot;significant element\u0026quot; of its revenue","growth from sales of chips designed for gaming, the SEC further added in a statement and charging order.","Those omissions misled investors and analysts who were interested in understanding the impact of","cryptomining on Nvidia\u0026#39;s business, the SEC emphasized. Nvidia, which did not admit or deny the","findings, has agreed to pay $5.5 million to settle civil charges, according to a statement made by the SEC","in May 2022.[254]","","French Competition Authority Investigation","On September 26, 2023, Nvidia\u0026#39;s French offices were searched by the French Competition Authority. The","raid, authorized by a judge, was part of an investigation into suspected anti-competitive practices in the","graphics card sector. Nvidia has not publicly commented on the incident.[255]","","AI regulation dispute with Anthropic","In July 2025, a public dispute emerged between Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang and Anthropic CEO Dario","Amodei over AI regulation and industry practices. The conflict escalated when Amodei vehemently","denied Huang\u0026#39;s allegations that he sought to control the AI industry through safety concerns, calling","Huang\u0026#39;s claims an \u0026quot;outrageous lie.\u0026quot;[256] The dispute centered on differing philosophies regarding AI","","","","development, with Amodei advocating for stronger regulatory oversight and \u0026quot;responsible scaling","policies,\u0026quot; while Huang promoted open-source development and criticized what Nvidia characterized as","\u0026quot;regulatory capture.\u0026quot;[256] Nvidia responded by stating that \u0026quot;lobbying for regulatory capture against open","source will only stifle innovation, make AI less safe and secure, and less democratic.\u0026quot;[256] The","controversy highlighted broader tensions within the AI industry between companies favoring rapid","development and those emphasizing safety measures and regulation.[256]","","Proposed Shanghai facility","In May 2025, U.S. senators Jim Banks and Elizabeth Warren criticized a proposed Nvidia facility in","Shanghai, saying that it \u0026quot;raises significant national security and economic security issues that warrant","serious review.\u0026quot;[257]","","H20 production halt (2025)","In August 2025, Nvidia ordered suppliers to halt production of its H20 AI chip following Chinese","government directives warning domestic companies against purchasing the processor due to security","concerns.[258][259] The company directed suppliers including Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing","Company, Samsung Electronics, and Amkor Technology to suspend work on the China-focused","processor.[260]","","The H20 was developed in late 2023 specifically for the Chinese market to comply with U.S. export","restrictions, featuring 96GB of HBM3 memory and 4.0 TB/s memory bandwidth—higher than the H100","—but with significantly reduced computational power at 296 TFLOPs compared to the H100\u0026#39;s 1979","TFLOPs.[261][262] Despite lower raw performance, the H20 demonstrated over 20% faster performance","than the H100 in large language model inference tasks due to architectural optimizations.[261][262]","","Prior to the production halt, Nvidia had placed substantial orders for the H20, including 300,000 units","from TSMC in July 2025, driven by strong demand from Chinese technology companies.[263] CEO","Jensen Huang denied allegations that the H20 contained security backdoors, stating the chips were","designed solely for commercial use.[264] The production suspension occurred as Nvidia was developing","the B30A, a new chip based on its Blackwell architecture intended to succeed the H20 in the Chinese","market.[265]","","See also","San <div id="LC96" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">At the time, Malachowsky and Priem were frustrated with</div>
<div id="LC97" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Sun&#39;s management and were looking to leave, but Huang was</div>

<div id="LC99" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">own division at LSI.[26] The three co-founders discussed a The Denny&#39;s roadside diner in San Jose,</div>
<div id="LC100" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">vision of the future which was so compelling that Huang California, where Nvidia&#39;s three co-</div>
<div id="LC101" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">decided to leave LSI[29] and become the chief executive founders agreed to start the company in</div>
<div id="LC102" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">officer of their new startup.[26] late 1992</div>
<div id="LC103" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC104" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The three co-founders envisioned graphics-based processing</div>
<div id="LC105" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">as the best trajectory for tackling challenges that had eluded</div>
<div id="LC106" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">general-purpose computing methods.[29] As Huang later</div>
<div id="LC107" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">explained: &quot;We also observed that video games were</div>
<div id="LC108" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">simultaneously one of the most computationally challenging</div>
<div id="LC109" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">problems and would have incredibly high sales volume.</div>
<div id="LC110" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Those two conditions don&#39;t happen very often. Video games</div>
<div id="LC111" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">was our killer app — a flywheel to reach large markets</div>
<div id="LC112" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">funding huge R&amp;D to solve massive computational</div>
<div id="LC113" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">problems.&quot;[29]</div>
<div id="LC114" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC115" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia&#39;s former headquarters which was</div>
<div id="LC116" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The first problem was who would quit first. Huang&#39;s wife, home to the company through most of its</div>
<div id="LC117" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Lori, did not want him to resign from LSI unless pre-AI period (still in use)</div>
<div id="LC118" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Malachowsky resigned from Sun at the same time, and</div>
<div id="LC119" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Malachowsky&#39;s wife, Melody, felt the same way about</div>
<div id="LC120" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC121" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC122" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC123" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Huang.[30] Priem broke that deadlock by resigning first from</div>
<div id="LC124" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Sun, effective December 31, 1992.[30] According to Priem,</div>
<div id="LC125" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">this put pressure on Huang and Malachowsky to not leave</div>
<div id="LC126" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">him to &quot;flail alone&quot;, so they gave notice too.[31] Huang left</div>
<div id="LC127" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">LSI and &quot;officially joined Priem on February 17&quot;, which was</div>
<div id="LC128" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">also Huang&#39;s 30th birthday, while Malachowsky left Sun in</div>
<div id="LC129" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">early March.[31] In early 1993, the three founders began</div>
<div id="LC130" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">working together on their new startup in Priem&#39;s townhouse</div>
<div id="LC131" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in Fremont, California.[32]</div>
<div id="LC132" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC133" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">With $40,000 in the bank, the company was born.[29] The</div>
<div id="LC134" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company subsequently received $20 million of venture capital</div>
<div id="LC135" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">funding from Sequoia Capital, Sutter Hill Ventures and</div>
<div id="LC136" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">others.[33]</div>
<div id="LC137" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC138" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">During the late 1990s, Nvidia was one of 70 startup</div>
<div id="LC139" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">companies pursuing the idea that graphics acceleration for Aerial view of Endeavor, the first of the</div>
<div id="LC140" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC141" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">two new Nvidia headquarters buildings, in</div>
<div id="LC142" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">video games was the path to the future.[25] Only two Santa Clara, California, in 2017</div>
<div id="LC143" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">survived: Nvidia and ATI Technologies, the latter of which</div>
<div id="LC144" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">merged into AMD.[25]</div>
<div id="LC145" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC146" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia initially had no name.[34] Priem&#39;s first idea was</div>
<div id="LC147" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">&quot;Primal Graphics&quot;, a syllabic abbreviation of two of the</div>
<div id="LC148" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">founders&#39; last names, but that left out Huang.[34] They soon</div>
<div id="LC149" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">discovered it was impossible to create a workable name with</div>
<div id="LC150" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">syllables from all three founders&#39; names, after considering</div>
<div id="LC151" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">&quot;Huaprimal&quot;, &quot;Prihuamal&quot;, &quot;Malluapri&quot;, etc.[34] The next idea</div>
<div id="LC152" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">came from Priem&#39;s idea for the name of Nvidia&#39;s first</div>
<div id="LC153" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">product.[34] Priem originally wanted to call it the &quot;GXNV&quot;, as Entrance of Endeavor headquarters</div>
<div id="LC154" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC155" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">building in 2018</div>
<div id="LC156" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in the &quot;next version&quot; of the GX graphics chips which he had</div>
<div id="LC157" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">worked on at Sun.[32] Then Huang told Priem to &quot;drop the</div>
<div id="LC158" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">GX&quot;, resulting in the name &quot;NV&quot;.[32] Priem made a list of words with the letters &quot;NV&quot; in them.[34] At one</div>
<div id="LC159" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">point, Malachowsky and Priem wanted to call the company NVision, but that name was already taken by</div>
<div id="LC160" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">a manufacturer of toilet paper.[26] Both Priem[34] and Huang have taken credit for coming up with the</div>
<div id="LC161" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">name Nvidia,[26] from &quot;invidia&quot;, the Latin word for &quot;envy&quot;.[29]</div>
<div id="LC162" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC163" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">After the company outgrew Priem&#39;s townhouse, its original headquarters office was in Sunnyvale,</div>
<div id="LC164" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">California.[29]</div>
<div id="LC165" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC166" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">First graphics accelerator</div>
<div id="LC167" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia&#39;s first graphics accelerator, the NV1, was designed to process quadrilateral primitives (forward</div>
<div id="LC168" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">texture mapping), a feature that set it apart from competitors, who preferred triangle primitives.[26]</div>
<div id="LC169" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC170" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">However, when Microsoft introduced the DirectX platform, it chose not to support any other graphics</div>
<div id="LC171" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">software and announced that its Direct3D API would exclusively support triangles.[26][35] As a result, the</div>
<div id="LC172" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">NV1 failed to gain traction in the market.[36]</div>
<div id="LC173" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC174" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC175" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC176" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia had also entered into a partnership with Sega to supply the graphics chip for the Dreamcast</div>
<div id="LC177" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">console and worked on the project for about a year. However, Nvidia&#39;s technology was already lagging</div>
<div id="LC178" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">behind competitors. This placed the company in a difficult position: continue working on a chip that was</div>
<div id="LC179" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">likely doomed to fail or abandon the project, risking financial collapse.[37]</div>
<div id="LC180" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC181" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In a pivotal moment, Sega&#39;s president, Shoichiro Irimajiri, visited Huang in person to inform him that</div>
<div id="LC182" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Sega had decided to choose another vendor for the Dreamcast. However, Irimajiri believed in Nvidia&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC183" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">potential and persuaded Sega&#39;s management to invest $5 million into the company. Huang later reflected</div>
<div id="LC184" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">that this funding was all that kept Nvidia afloat, and that Irimajiri&#39;s &quot;understanding and generosity gave us</div>
<div id="LC185" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">six months to live&quot;.[37]</div>
<div id="LC186" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC187" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 1996, Huang laid off more than half of Nvidia&#39;s employees—thereby reducing headcount from 100 to</div>
<div id="LC188" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">40—and focused the company&#39;s remaining resources on developing a graphics accelerator product</div>
<div id="LC189" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">optimized for processing triangle primitives: the RIVA 128.[26][35] By the time the RIVA 128 was</div>
<div id="LC190" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">released in August 1997, Nvidia had only enough money left for one month&#39;s payroll.[26] The sense of</div>
<div id="LC191" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">impending failure became so pervasive that it gave rise to Nvidia&#39;s unofficial company motto: &quot;Our</div>
<div id="LC192" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company is thirty days from going out of business.&quot;[26] Huang began internal presentations to Nvidia staff</div>
<div id="LC193" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">with those words for many years.[26]</div>
<div id="LC194" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC195" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia sold about a million RIVA 128 units within four months,[26] and used the revenue to fund</div>
<div id="LC196" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">development of its next generation of products.[35] In 1998, the release of the RIVA TNT helped solidify</div>
<div id="LC197" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia&#39;s reputation as a leader in graphics technology.[38]</div>
<div id="LC198" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC199" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Public company</div>
<div id="LC200" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia went public on January 22, 1999.[39][40][41] Investing in Nvidia after it had already failed to</div>
<div id="LC201" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">deliver on its contract turned out to be Irimajiri&#39;s best decision as Sega&#39;s president. After Irimajiri left</div>
<div id="LC202" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Sega in 2000, Sega sold its Nvidia stock for $15 million.[37]</div>
<div id="LC203" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC204" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In late 1999, Nvidia released the GeForce 256 (NV10), its first product expressly marketed as a GPU,</div>
<div id="LC205" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">which was most notable for introducing onboard transformation and lighting (T&amp;L) to consumer-level</div>
<div id="LC206" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">3D hardware. Running at 120 MHz and featuring four-pixel pipelines, it implemented advanced video</div>
<div id="LC207" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">acceleration, motion compensation, and hardware sub-picture alpha blending. The GeForce outperformed</div>
<div id="LC208" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">existing products by a wide margin.</div>
<div id="LC209" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC210" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Due to the success of its products, Nvidia won the contract to develop the graphics hardware for</div>
<div id="LC211" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft&#39;s Xbox game console, which earned Nvidia a $200 million advance. However, the project took</div>
<div id="LC212" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">many of its best engineers away from other projects. In the short term this did not matter, and the</div>
<div id="LC213" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">GeForce 2 GTS shipped in the summer of 2000. In December 2000, Nvidia reached an agreement to</div>
<div id="LC214" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">acquire the intellectual assets of its one-time rival 3dfx, a pioneer in consumer 3D graphics technology</div>
<div id="LC215" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">leading the field from the mid-1990s until 2000.[42][43] The acquisition process was finalized in April</div>
<div id="LC216" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2002.[44]</div>
<div id="LC217" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC218" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2001, Standard &amp; Poor&#39;s selected Nvidia to replace the departing Enron in the S&amp;P 500 stock index,</div>
<div id="LC219" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">meaning that index funds would need to hold Nvidia shares going forward.[45]</div>
<div id="LC220" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC221" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC222" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC223" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In July 2002, Nvidia acquired Exluna for an undisclosed sum. Exluna made software-rendering tools and</div>
<div id="LC224" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the personnel were merged into the Cg project.[46] In August 2003, Nvidia acquired MediaQ for</div>
<div id="LC225" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">approximately US$70 million.[47] It launched GoForce the following year. On April 22, 2004, Nvidia</div>
<div id="LC226" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">acquired iReady, also a provider of high-performance TCP offload engines and iSCSI controllers.[48] In</div>
<div id="LC227" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">December 2004, it was announced that Nvidia would assist Sony with the design of the graphics</div>
<div id="LC228" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">processor (RSX) for the PlayStation 3 game console. On December 14, 2005, Nvidia acquired ULI</div>
<div id="LC229" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Electronics, which at the time supplied third-party southbridge parts for chipsets to ATI, Nvidia&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC230" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">competitor.[49] In March 2006, Nvidia acquired Hybrid Graphics.[50] In December 2006, Nvidia, along</div>
<div id="LC231" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">with its main rival in the graphics industry AMD (which had acquired ATI), received subpoenas from the</div>
<div id="LC232" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">U.S. Department of Justice regarding possible antitrust violations in the graphics card industry.[51]</div>
<div id="LC233" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC234" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2007–2014</div>
<div id="LC235" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Forbes named Nvidia its Company of the Year for 2007, citing the accomplishments it made during the</div>
<div id="LC236" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">said period as well as during the previous five years.[52] On January 5, 2007, Nvidia announced that it</div>
<div id="LC237" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">had completed the acquisition of PortalPlayer, Inc.[53] In February 2008, Nvidia acquired Ageia,</div>
<div id="LC238" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">developer of PhysX, a physics engine and physics processing unit. Nvidia announced that it planned to</div>
<div id="LC239" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">integrate the PhysX technology into its future GPU products.[54][55]</div>
<div id="LC240" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC241" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In July 2008, Nvidia took a write-down of approximately $200 million on its first-quarter revenue, after</div>
<div id="LC242" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">reporting that certain mobile chipsets and GPUs produced by the company had &quot;abnormal failure rates&quot;</div>
<div id="LC243" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">due to manufacturing defects. Nvidia, however, did not reveal the affected products. In September 2008,</div>
<div id="LC244" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia became the subject of a class action lawsuit over the defects, claiming that the faulty GPUs had</div>
<div id="LC245" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">been incorporated into certain laptop models manufactured by Apple Inc., Dell, and HP. In September</div>
<div id="LC246" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2010, Nvidia reached a settlement, in which it would reimburse owners of the affected laptops for repairs</div>
<div id="LC247" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">or, in some cases, replacement.[56][57] On January 10, 2011, Nvidia signed a six-year, $1.5 billion cross-</div>
<div id="LC248" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">licensing agreement with Intel, ending all litigation between the two companies.[58]</div>
<div id="LC249" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC250" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In November 2011, after initially unveiling it at Mobile World Congress, Nvidia released its ARM-based</div>
<div id="LC251" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">system on a chip for mobile devices, Tegra 3. Nvidia claimed that the chip featured the first-ever quad-</div>
<div id="LC252" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">core mobile CPU.[59][60] In May 2011, it was announced that Nvidia had agreed to acquire Icera, a</div>
<div id="LC253" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">baseband chip making company in the UK, for $367 million.[61] In January 2013, Nvidia unveiled the</div>
<div id="LC254" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Tegra 4, as well as the Nvidia Shield, an Android-based handheld game console powered by the new</div>
<div id="LC255" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">system on a chip.[62] On July 29, 2013, Nvidia announced that they acquired PGI from</div>
<div id="LC256" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">STMicroelectronics.[63]</div>
<div id="LC257" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC258" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In February 2013, Nvidia announced its plans to build a new headquarters in the form of two giant</div>
<div id="LC259" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">triangle-shaped buildings on the other side of San Tomas Expressway (to the west of its existing</div>
<div id="LC260" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">headquarters complex). The company selected triangles as its design theme. As Huang explained in a</div>
<div id="LC261" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">blog post, the triangle is &quot;the fundamental building block of computer graphics&quot;.[64]</div>
<div id="LC262" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC263" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2014, Nvidia ported the Valve games Portal and Half Life 2 to its Nvidia Shield Tablet as Lightspeed</div>
<div id="LC264" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Studio.[65][66] Since 2014, Nvidia has diversified its business focusing on three markets: gaming,</div>
<div id="LC265" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">automotive electronics, and mobile devices.[67]</div>
<div id="LC266" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC267" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">That same year, Nvidia also prevailed in litigation brought by the trustee of 3dfx&#39;s bankruptcy estate to</div>
<div id="LC268" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">challenge its 2000 acquisition of 3dfx&#39;s intellectual assets. On November 6, 2014, in an unpublished</div>
<div id="LC269" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">memorandum order, the U.S. Court of Appeals for the Ninth Circuit affirmed the &quot;district court&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC270" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC271" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC272" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC273" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">judgment affirming the bankruptcy court&#39;s determination that [Nvidia] did not pay less than fair market</div>
<div id="LC274" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">value for assets purchased from 3dfx shortly before 3dfx filed for bankruptcy&quot;.[68]</div>
<div id="LC275" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC276" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2016–2018</div>
<div id="LC277" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On May 6, 2016, Nvidia unveiled the first GPUs of the GeForce 10 series, the GTX 1080 and 1070,</div>
<div id="LC278" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">based on the company&#39;s new Pascal microarchitecture. Nvidia claimed that both models outperformed its</div>
<div id="LC279" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Maxwell-based Titan X model; the models incorporate GDDR5X and GDDR5 memory respectively, and</div>
<div id="LC280" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">use a 16  nm manufacturing process. The architecture also supports a new hardware feature known as</div>
<div id="LC281" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">simultaneous multi-projection (SMP), which is designed to improve the quality of multi-monitor and</div>
<div id="LC282" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">virtual reality (VR) rendering.[69][70][71] Laptops that include these GPUs and are sufficiently thin – as of</div>
<div id="LC283" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">late 2017, under 0.8 inches (20  mm)  – have been designated as meeting Nvidia&#39;s &quot;Max-Q&quot; design</div>
<div id="LC284" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">standard.[72]</div>
<div id="LC285" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC286" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In July 2016, Nvidia agreed to a settlement for a false advertising lawsuit regarding its GTX 970 model,</div>
<div id="LC287" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">as the models were unable to use all of their advertised 4 GB of VRAM due to limitations brought by the</div>
<div id="LC288" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">design of its hardware.[73] In May 2017, Nvidia announced a partnership with Toyota which would use</div>
<div id="LC289" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia&#39;s Drive PX-series artificial intelligence platform for its autonomous vehicles.[74] In July 2017,</div>
<div id="LC290" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia and Chinese search giant Baidu announced a far-reaching AI partnership that includes cloud</div>
<div id="LC291" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">computing, autonomous driving, consumer devices, and Baidu&#39;s open-source AI framework</div>
<div id="LC292" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">PaddlePaddle. Baidu unveiled that Nvidia&#39;s Drive PX 2 AI will be the foundation of its autonomous-</div>
<div id="LC293" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">vehicle platform.[75]</div>
<div id="LC294" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC295" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia officially released the Titan V on December 7, 2017.[76][77]</div>
<div id="LC296" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC297" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia officially released the Nvidia Quadro GV100 on March 27, 2018.[78] Nvidia officially released the</div>
<div id="LC298" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">RTX 2080 GPUs on September 27, 2018. In 2018, Google announced that Nvidia&#39;s Tesla P4 graphic</div>
<div id="LC299" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">cards would be integrated into Google Cloud service&#39;s artificial intelligence.[79]</div>
<div id="LC300" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC301" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In May 2018, on the Nvidia user forum, a thread was started[80] asking the company to update users when</div>
<div id="LC302" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">they would release web drivers for its cards installed on legacy Mac Pro machines up to mid-2012 5,1</div>
<div id="LC303" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">running the macOS Mojave operating system 10.14. Web drivers are required to enable graphics</div>
<div id="LC304" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">acceleration and multiple display monitor capabilities of the GPU. On its Mojave update info website,</div>
<div id="LC305" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Apple stated that macOS Mojave would run on legacy machines with &#39;Metal compatible&#39; graphics</div>
<div id="LC306" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">cards[81] and listed Metal compatible GPUs, including some manufactured by Nvidia.[82] However, this</div>
<div id="LC307" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">list did not include Metal compatible cards that currently work in macOS High Sierra using Nvidia-</div>
<div id="LC308" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">developed web drivers. In September, Nvidia responded, &quot;Apple fully controls drivers for macOS. But if</div>
<div id="LC309" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Apple allows, our engineers are ready and eager to help Apple deliver great drivers for macOS 10.14</div>
<div id="LC310" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">(Mojave).&quot;[83] In October, Nvidia followed this up with another public announcement, &quot;Apple fully</div>
<div id="LC311" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">controls drivers for macOS. Unfortunately, Nvidia currently cannot release a driver unless it is approved</div>
<div id="LC312" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">by Apple,&quot;[84] suggesting a possible rift between the two companies.[85] By January 2019, with still no</div>
<div id="LC313" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">sign of the enabling web drivers, Apple Insider weighed into the controversy with a claim that Apple</div>
<div id="LC314" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">management &quot;doesn&#39;t want Nvidia support in macOS&quot;.[86] The following month, Apple Insider followed</div>
<div id="LC315" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">this up with another claim that Nvidia support was abandoned because of &quot;relational issues in the</div>
<div id="LC316" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">past&quot;,[87] and that Apple was developing its own GPU technology.[88] Without Apple-approved Nvidia</div>
<div id="LC317" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">web drivers, Apple users are faced with replacing their Nvidia cards with a competing supported brand,</div>
<div id="LC318" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">such as AMD Radeon from the list recommended by Apple.[89]</div>
<div id="LC319" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC320" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC321" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC322" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2019 acquisition of Mellanox Technologies</div>
<div id="LC323" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On March 11, 2019, Nvidia announced a deal to buy</div>
<div id="LC324" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Mellanox Technologies for $6.9 billion[90] to substantially</div>
<div id="LC325" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">expand its footprint in the high-performance computing</div>
<div id="LC326" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">market. In May 2019, Nvidia announced new RTX Studio</div>
<div id="LC327" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">laptops. The creators say that the new laptop is going to be</div>
<div id="LC328" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">seven times faster than a top-end MacBook Pro with a Core i9</div>
<div id="LC329" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and AMD&#39;s Radeon Pro Vega 20 graphics in apps like Maya</div>
<div id="LC330" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and RedCine-X Pro.[91] In August 2019, Nvidia announced</div>

<div id="LC332" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">game Minecraft adding real-time DXR ray tracing exclusively Nvidia Yokneam office (former Mellanox</div>

<div id="LC336" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">work inside the engine.[92]</div>
<div id="LC337" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">

<div id="LC338" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2020–2023</div>
<div id="LC339" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In May 2020, Nvidia announced it was acquiring Cumulus Networks.[93] Post acquisition the company</div>
<div id="LC340" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">was absorbed into Nvidia&#39;s networking business unit, along with Mellanox.</div>

</div>
<div id="LC342" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In May 2020, Nvidia&#39;s developed an open-source ventilator to address the shortage resulting from the</div>
<div id="LC343" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">global coronavirus pandemic.[94] On May 14, 2020, Nvidia officially announced their Ampere GPU</div>
<div id="LC344" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">microarchitecture and the Nvidia A100 GPU accelerator.[95][96] In July 2020, it was reported that Nvidia</div>
<div id="LC345" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">was in talks with SoftBank to buy Arm, a UK-based chip designer, for $32 billion.[97]</div>
<div id="LC346" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC347" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On September 1, 2020, Nvidia officially announced the GeForce 30 series based on the company&#39;s new</div>
<div id="LC348" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Ampere microarchitecture.[98][99]</div>
<div id="LC349" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC350" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On September 13, 2020, Nvidia announced that they would buy Arm from SoftBank Group for $40</div>
<div id="LC351" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">billion, subject to the usual scrutiny, with the latter retaining a 10% share of Nvidia.[100][101][102][103]</div>

</div>
<div id="LC353" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In October 2020, Nvidia announced its plan to build the most</div>
<div id="LC354" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">powerful computer in Cambridge, England. The computer,</div>
<div id="LC355" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">called Cambridge-1, launched in July 2021 with a $100</div>
<div id="LC356" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">million investment and will employ AI to support healthcare</div>
<div id="LC357" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">research.[104][105] According to Jensen Huang, &quot;The</div>
<div id="LC358" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Cambridge-1 supercomputer will serve as a hub of innovation</div>
<div id="LC359" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">for the UK, and further the groundbreaking work being done</div>
<div id="LC360" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">by the nation&#39;s researchers in critical healthcare and drug Nvidia GeForce RTX 2080 Ti, part of the</div>
<div id="LC361" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">discovery.&quot;[106] RTX 20 series, which is the first</div>
<div id="LC362" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC363" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">generation of Nvidia RTX</div>
<div id="LC364" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Also in October 2020, along with the release of the Nvidia</div>
<div id="LC365" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">RTX A6000, Nvidia announced it is retiring its workstation</div>

<div id="LC368" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">

<div id="LC369" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC370" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC371" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In August 2021, the proposed takeover of Arm was stalled after the UK&#39;s Competition and Markets</div>
<div id="LC372" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Authority raised &quot;significant competition concerns&quot;.[108] In October 2021, the European Commission</div>
<div id="LC373" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">opened a competition investigation into the takeover. The Commission stated that Nvidia&#39;s acquisition</div>
<div id="LC374" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">could restrict competitors&#39; access to Arm&#39;s products and provide Nvidia with too much internal</div>
<div id="LC375" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">information on its competitors due to their deals with Arm. SoftBank (the parent company of Arm) and</div>
<div id="LC376" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia announced in early February 2022 that they &quot;had agreed not to move forward with the transaction</div>
<div id="LC377" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">&#39;because of significant regulatory challenges&#39; &quot;.[109] The investigation was set to end on March 15,</div>
<div id="LC378" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2022.[110][111] That same month, Nvidia was reportedly compromised by a cyberattack.[112] This would</div>
<div id="LC379" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">have been the largest semiconductor acquisition in history.[17][18]</div>
<div id="LC380" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC381" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In March 2022, Nvidia&#39;s CEO Jensen Huang mentioned that they were open to having Intel manufacture</div>
<div id="LC382" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">their chips in the future.[113] This was the first time the company mentioned that they would work</div>
<div id="LC383" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">together with Intel&#39;s upcoming foundry services.</div>

<div id="LC386" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Armenia.[114]</div>

<div id="LC393" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC394" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In September 2022, Nvidia announced a collaboration with the Broad Institute of MIT and Harvard</div>
<div id="LC395" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">related to the entire suite of Nvidia&#39;s AI-powered healthcare software suite called Clara, that includes</div>
<div id="LC396" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Parabricks and MONAI.[119]</div>

</div>
<div id="LC398" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Following U.S. Department of Commerce regulations which placed an embargo on exports to China of</div>
<div id="LC399" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">advanced microchips, which went into effect in October 2022, Nvidia saw its data center chip added to</div>
<div id="LC400" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the export control list. The next month, the company unveiled a new advanced chip in China, called the</div>
<div id="LC401" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A800 GPU, that met the export control rules.[120]</div>

<div id="LC404" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">by Getty Images, a new tool that lets people create images using Getty&#39;s library of licensed photos. Getty</div>
<div id="LC405" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">will use Nvidia&#39;s Edify model, which is available on Nvidia&#39;s generative AI model library Picasso.[121]</div>
<div id="LC406" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC407" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On September 26, 2023, Denny&#39;s CEO Kelli Valade joined Huang in East San Jose to celebrate the</div>
<div id="LC408" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">founding of Nvidia at Denny&#39;s on Berryessa Road, where a plaque was installed to mark the relevant</div>
<div id="LC409" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">corner booth as the birthplace of a $1 trillion company.[26][122] By then, Nvidia&#39;s H100 GPUs were in</div>
<div id="LC410" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">such demand that even other tech giants were beholden to how Nvidia allocated supply. Larry Ellison of</div>

<div id="LC412" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Musk of Tesla, Inc. and xAI &quot;were begging&quot; for H100s, &quot;I guess is the best way to describe it. An hour of</div>
<div id="LC413" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">sushi and begging&quot;.[123]</div>

</div>
<div id="LC415" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In October 2023, it was reported that Nvidia had quietly begun designing ARM-based central processing</div>
<div id="LC416" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">units (CPUs) for Microsoft&#39;s Windows operating system with a target to start selling them in 2025.[124]</div>
<div id="LC417" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">

</div>
<div id="LC420" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2024–2025</div>
<div id="LC421" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In January 2024, Forbes reported that Nvidia has increased its lobbying presence in Washington, D.C. as</div>
<div id="LC422" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">American lawmakers consider proposals to regulate artificial intelligence. From 2023 to 2024, the</div>
<div id="LC423" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company reportedly hired at least four government affairs with professional backgrounds at agencies</div>
<div id="LC424" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">including the United States Department of State and the Department of the Treasury. It was noted that the</div>
<div id="LC425" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">$350,000 spent by the company on lobbying in 2023 was small compared to a number of major tech</div>
<div id="LC426" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">companies in the artificial intelligence space.[125]</div>
<div id="LC427" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC428" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In January 2024, Raymond James Financial analysts estimated that Nvidia was selling the H100 GPU in</div>
<div id="LC429" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the price range of $25,000 to $30,000 each, while on eBay, individual H100s cost over $40,000.[126]</div>
<div id="LC430" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC431" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Several major technology companies were purchasing tens or hundreds of thousands of GPUs for their</div>
<div id="LC432" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">data centers to run generative artificial intelligence projects; simple arithmetic implied that they were</div>
<div id="LC433" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">committing to billions of dollars in capital expenditures.[126]</div>
<div id="LC434" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC435" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In February 2024, it was reported that Nvidia was the &quot;hot employer&quot; in Silicon Valley because it was</div>
<div id="LC436" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">offering interesting work and good pay at a time when other tech employers were downsizing. Half of</div>
<div id="LC437" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia employees earned over $228,000 in 2023.[127] By then, Nvidia GPUs had become so valuable that</div>
<div id="LC438" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">they needed special security while in transit to data centers. Cisco chief information officer Fletcher</div>
<div id="LC439" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Previn explained at a CIO summit: &quot;Those GPUs arrive by armored car&quot;.[128]</div>
