</div>
<div id="LC441" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On March 1, 2024, Nvidia became the third company in the history of the United States to close with a</div>
<div id="LC442" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">market capitalization in excess of $2 trillion.[45] Nvidia needed only 180 days to get to $2 trillion from $1</div>
<div id="LC443" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">trillion, while the first two companies, Apple and Microsoft, each took over 500 days.[45] On March 18,</div>
<div id="LC444" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia announced its new AI chip and microarchitecture Blackwell, named after mathematician David</div>
<div id="LC445" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Blackwell.[129]</div>
<div id="LC446" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC447" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In April 2024, Reuters reported that China had allegedly acquired banned Nvidia chips and servers from</div>
<div id="LC448" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Supermicro and Dell via tenders.[130]</div>
<div id="LC449" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC450" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In June 2024, the Federal Trade Commission (FTC) and the Justice Department (DOJ) began antitrust</div>
<div id="LC451" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">investigations into Nvidia, Microsoft and OpenAI, focusing on their influence in the AI industry. The</div>
<div id="LC452" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">FTC led the investigations into Microsoft and OpenAI, while the DOJ handled Nvidia. The probes</div>
<div id="LC453" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">centered on the companies&#39; conduct rather than mergers. This development followed an open letter from</div>
<div id="LC454" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">OpenAI employees expressing concerns about the rapid AI advancements and lack of oversight.[131]</div>
<div id="LC455" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC456" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The company became the world&#39;s most valuable, surpassing Microsoft and Apple, on June 18, 2024, after</div>
<div id="LC457" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">its market capitalization exceeded $3.3 trillion.[132][133]</div>
<div id="LC458" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC459" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In June 2024, Trend Micro announced a partnership with Nvidia to develop AI-driven security tools,</div>
<div id="LC460" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">notably to protect the data centers where AI workloads are processed. This collaboration integrates</div>
<div id="LC461" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia NIM and Nvidia Morpheus with Trend Vision One and its Sovereign and Private Cloud solutions</div>
<div id="LC462" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">to improve data privacy, real-time analysis, and rapid threat mitigation.[134]</div>
<div id="LC463" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC464" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In October 2024, Nvidia introduced a family of open-source multimodal large language models called</div>
<div id="LC465" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">NVLM 1.0, which features a flagship version with 72 billion parameters, designed to improve text-only</div>
<div id="LC466" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">performance after multimodal training.[135][136]</div>
<div id="LC467" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC468" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In November 2024, the company was added to the Dow Jones Industrial Average.[137][138]</div>
<div id="LC469" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC470" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC471" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC472" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In November 2024, Morgan Stanley reported that &quot;the entire 2025 production&quot; of all of Nvidia&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC473" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Blackwell chips was &quot;already sold out&quot;.[139]</div>
<div id="LC474" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC475" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Also in November 2024, the company bought 1.2 million shares of Nebius Group.[140]</div>
<div id="LC476" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC477" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia was ranked #3 on Forbes&#39; &quot;Best Places to Work&quot; list in 2024.[141]</div>
<div id="LC478" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC479" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">As of January 7, 2025, Nvidia&#39;s $3.66 trillion market cap was worth more than double of the combined</div>
<div id="LC480" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">value of AMD, ARM, Broadcom, and Intel.[142]</div>
<div id="LC481" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC482" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In January 2025, Nvidia saw the largest one-day loss in market capitalization for a U.S. company in</div>
<div id="LC483" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">history at $600 billion. This was due to DeepSeek, a Chinese AI startup that developed an advanced AI</div>
<div id="LC484" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">model at a lower cost and computing power.[143] DeepSeek&#39;s AI assistant, using the V3 model, surpassed</div>
<div id="LC485" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">ChatGPT as the highest-rated free app in the U.S. on Apple&#39;s App Store.[144][145]</div>
<div id="LC486" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC487" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On April 7, 2025, Nvidia released the Llama-3.1-Nemotron-Ultra-253B-v1 reasoning large language</div>
<div id="LC488" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">model, under the Nvidia Open Model License. It comes in three sizes: Nano, Super and Ultra.[146]</div>
<div id="LC489" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC490" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On May 28, 2025, Nvidia&#39;s second-quarter revenue forecast fell short of market estimates due to U.S.</div>
<div id="LC491" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">export restrictions impacting AI chip sales to China, yet the company&#39;s stock rose 5% as investors</div>
<div id="LC492" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">remained optimistic about long-term AI demand.[147]</div>
<div id="LC493" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC494" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In July 2025, it was announced that Nvidia had acquired CentL, a Canadian-based AI firm.[148]</div>
<div id="LC495" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC496" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On July 10, 2025, Nvidia closed for the first time with a market cap above $4 trillion, after its market cap</div>
<div id="LC497" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">briefly touched and then retreated from that number during the previous day.[149] Nvidia became the first</div>
<div id="LC498" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company to reach a market cap of $4 trillion.[150] At that point, Nvidia was worth more than the</div>
<div id="LC499" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">combined value of all publicly traded companies in the United Kingdom.[149]</div>
<div id="LC500" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC501" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On July 29, 2025, Nvidia ordered 300,000 H20 AI chips from Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing</div>
<div id="LC502" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Company (TSMC) due to strong demand from Chinese tech firms like Tencent and Alibaba.[151]</div>
<div id="LC503" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC504" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In August 2025, Nvidia and competitor Advanced Micro Devices agreed to pay 15% of the revenues from</div>
<div id="LC505" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">certain chip sales in China as part of an arrangement to obtain export licenses.[152] Nvidia will pay only</div>
<div id="LC506" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">for sales of the H20 chips.[153]</div>
<div id="LC507" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC508" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Fabless manufacturing</div>
<div id="LC509" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia uses external suppliers for all phases of manufacturing, including wafer fabrication, assembly,</div>
<div id="LC510" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">testing, and packaging. Nvidia thus avoids most of the investment and production costs and risks</div>
<div id="LC511" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">associated with chip manufacturing, although it does sometimes directly procure some components and</div>
<div id="LC512" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">materials used in the production of its products (e.g., memory and substrates). Nvidia focuses its own</div>
<div id="LC513" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">resources on product design, quality assurance, marketing, and customer support.[154][155]</div>
<div id="LC514" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC515" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC516" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">

<div id="LC559" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Finances</div>
<div id="LC560" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">For the fiscal year 2020, Nvidia reported earnings of US$2.796 billion, with an annual revenue of</div>
<div id="LC561" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">US$10.918 billion, a decline of 6.8% over the previous fiscal cycle. Nvidia&#39;s shares traded at over $531</div>
<div id="LC562" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">per share, and its market capitalization was valued at over US$328.7 billion in January 2021. [159][160]</div>

</div>

</div>
<div id="LC566" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">For the Q2 of 2020, Nvidia reported sales of $3.87 billion,</div>
<div id="LC567" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">which was a 50% rise from the same period in 2019. The</div>
<div id="LC568" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">surge in sales and people&#39;s higher demand for computer</div>
<div id="LC569" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">technology. According to the financial chief of the</div>
<div id="LC570" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company, Colette Kress, the effects of the pandemic will</div>
<div id="LC571" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">&quot;likely reflect this evolution in enterprise workforce trends</div>
<div id="LC572" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">with a greater focus on technologies, such as Nvidia</div>
<div id="LC573" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">laptops and virtual workstations, that enable remote work Nvidia stock price (1999–2023)</div>
<div id="LC574" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and virtual collaboration.&quot;[161] In May 2023, Nvidia</div>

<div id="LC605" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">GPU Technology Conference</div>
<div id="LC606" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia&#39;s GPU Technology Conference (GTC) is a series of technical conferences held around the</div>
<div id="LC607" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">world.[164] It originated in 2009 in San Jose, California, with an initial focus on the potential for solving</div>
<div id="LC608" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">computing challenges through GPUs.[165] In recent years, the conference&#39;s focus has shifted to various</div>
<div id="LC609" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">applications of artificial intelligence and deep learning; including self-driving cars, healthcare, high-</div>
<div id="LC610" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">performance computing, and Nvidia Deep Learning Institute (DLI) training.[166] GTC 2018 attracted over</div>
<div id="LC611" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">8400 attendees.[164] GTC 2020 was converted to a digital event and drew roughly 59,000 registrants.[167]</div>
<div id="LC612" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC613" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">After several years of remote-only events, GTC in March 2024 returned to an in-person format in San</div>
<div id="LC614" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Jose, California.[168]</div>
<div id="LC615" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC616" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Product families</div>
<div id="LC617" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia&#39;s product families include graphics processing units, wireless communication devices, and</div>
<div id="LC618" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">automotive hardware and software, such as:</div>
<div id="LC619" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC620" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">GeForce, consumer-oriented graphics processing products</div>
<div id="LC621" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC622" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC623" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC624" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">RTX, professional visual computing graphics</div>
<div id="LC625" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">processing products (replacing GTX and Quadro)</div>
<div id="LC626" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">NVS, a multi-display business graphics processor</div>
<div id="LC627" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Tegra, a system on a chip series for mobile devices</div>
<div id="LC628" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Tesla, line of dedicated general-purpose GPUs for</div>
<div id="LC629" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">high-end image generation applications in</div>
<div id="LC630" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">professional and scientific fields</div>
<div id="LC631" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">nForce, a motherboard chipset created by Nvidia for</div>
<div id="LC632" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Intel (Celeron, Pentium and Core 2) and AMD</div>
<div id="LC633" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">(Athlon and Duron) microprocessors A Shield Tablet with its accompanying</div>
<div id="LC634" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">GRID, a set of hardware and services by Nvidia for input pen (left) and gamepad</div>
<div id="LC635" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">graphics virtualization</div>
<div id="LC636" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Shield, a range of gaming hardware including the</div>
<div id="LC637" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Shield Portable, Shield Tablet and Shield TV</div>
<div id="LC638" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Drive, a range of hardware and software products for designers and manufacturers of</div>
<div id="LC639" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">autonomous vehicles. The Drive PX-series is a high-performance computer platform aimed</div>
<div id="LC640" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">at autonomous driving through deep learning,[169] while Driveworks is an operating system</div>
<div id="LC641" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">for driverless cars.[170]</div>
<div id="LC642" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC643" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">BlueField, a range of data processing units, initially inherited from their acquisition of</div>
<div id="LC644" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Mellanox Technologies[171][172]</div>
<div id="LC645" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC646" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Datacenter/server class CPU, codenamed Grace, released in 2023[173][174]</div>
<div id="LC647" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC648" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">DGX, an enterprise platform designed for deep learning applications</div>
<div id="LC649" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Maxine, a platform providing developers a suite of AI-based conferencing software[175]</div>
<div id="LC650" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC651" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Open-source software support</div>
<div id="LC652" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Until September 23, 2013, Nvidia had not published any documentation for its advanced hardware,[176]</div>
<div id="LC653" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC654" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">meaning that programmers could not write free and open-source device drivers for its products without</div>
<div id="LC655" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">resorting to reverse engineering.</div>
<div id="LC656" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC657" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Instead, Nvidia provides its own binary GeForce graphics drivers for X.Org and an open-source library</div>
<div id="LC658" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">that interfaces with the Linux, FreeBSD or Solaris kernels and the proprietary graphics software. Nvidia</div>
<div id="LC659" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">also provided but stopped supporting an obfuscated open-source driver that only supports two-</div>
<div id="LC660" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">dimensional hardware acceleration and ships with the X.Org distribution.[177]</div>
<div id="LC661" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC662" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The proprietary nature of Nvidia&#39;s drivers has generated dissatisfaction within free-software communities.</div>
<div id="LC663" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In a 2012 talk, Linus Torvalds gave a middle-finger gesture and criticized Nvidia’s stance toward</div>
<div id="LC664" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Linux.&quot;[178][179] Some Linux and BSD users insist on using only open-source drivers and regard Nvidia&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC665" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">insistence on providing nothing more than a binary-only driver as inadequate, given that competing</div>
<div id="LC666" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">manufacturers such as Intel offer support and documentation for open-source developers, and others like</div>
<div id="LC667" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">AMD release partial documentation and provide some active development.[180][181]</div>
<div id="LC668" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC669" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia only provides x86/x64 and ARMv7-A versions of their proprietary driver; as a result, features like</div>
<div id="LC670" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">CUDA are unavailable on other platforms.[182] Some users claim that Nvidia&#39;s Linux drivers impose</div>
<div id="LC671" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">artificial restrictions, like limiting the number of monitors that can be used at the same time, but the</div>
<div id="LC672" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company has not commented on these accusations.[183]</div>
<div id="LC673" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC674" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC675" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC676" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2014, with its Maxwell GPUs, Nvidia started to require firmware by them to unlock all features of its</div>
<div id="LC677" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">graphics cards.[184][185][186]</div>
<div id="LC678" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC679" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On May 12, 2022, Nvidia announced that they are opensourcing their GPU kernel modules.[187][188][189]</div>
<div id="LC680" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC681" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Support for Nvidia&#39;s firmware was implemented in nouveau in 2023, which allows proper power</div>
<div id="LC682" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">management and GPU reclocking for Turing and newer graphics card generations.[190][191]</div>
<div id="LC683" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC684" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 21 July 2025, Nvidia announce to extend CUDA support to RISC-V.[192][193][194]</div>
<div id="LC685" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC686" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">List of Nvidia open-source projects</div>
<div id="LC687" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nouveau</div>
<div id="LC688" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">NVDLA</div>
<div id="LC689" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">PhysX</div>
<div id="LC690" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">VDPAU</div>
<div id="LC691" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Vibrante</div>
<div id="LC692" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">GR00T[195]</div>
<div id="LC693" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC694" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Deep learning</div>
<div id="LC695" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia GPUs are used in deep learning, and accelerated analytics due to Nvidia&#39;s CUDA software</div>
<div id="LC696" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">platform and API which allows programmers to utilize the higher number of cores present in GPUs to</div>
<div id="LC697" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">parallelize BLAS operations which are extensively used in machine learning algorithms.[11] They were</div>
<div id="LC698" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">included in many Tesla, Inc. vehicles before Musk announced at Tesla Autonomy Day in 2019 that the</div>
<div id="LC699" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company developed its own SoC and full self-driving computer now and would stop using Nvidia</div>
<div id="LC700" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">hardware for their vehicles.[196][197] These GPUs are used by researchers, laboratories, tech companies</div>
<div id="LC701" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and enterprise companies.[198] In 2009, Nvidia was involved in what was called the &quot;big bang&quot; of deep</div>
<div id="LC702" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">learning, &quot;as deep-learning neural networks were combined with Nvidia graphics processing units</div>
<div id="LC703" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">(GPUs)&quot;.[199] That year, the Google Brain team used Nvidia GPUs to create deep neural networks</div>
<div id="LC704" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">capable of machine learning, where Andrew Ng determined that GPUs could increase the speed of deep</div>
<div id="LC705" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">learning systems by about 100 times.[200]</div>
<div id="LC706" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC707" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">DGX</div>
<div id="LC708" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">DGX is a line of supercomputers by Nvidia.</div>
<div id="LC709" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC710" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In April 2016, Nvidia produced the DGX-1 based on an 8 GPU cluster, to improve the ability of users to</div>
<div id="LC711" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">use deep learning by combining GPUs with integrated deep learning software.[201] Nvidia gifted its first</div>
<div id="LC712" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">DGX-1 to OpenAI in August 2016 to help it train larger and more complex AI models with the capability</div>
<div id="LC713" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of reducing processing time from six days to two hours.[202][203] It also developed Nvidia Tesla K80 and</div>
<div id="LC714" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">P100 GPU-based virtual machines, which are available through Google Cloud, which Google installed in</div>
<div id="LC715" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">November 2016.[204] Microsoft added GPU servers in a preview offering of its N series based on Nvidia&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC716" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Tesla K80s, each containing 4992 processing cores. Later that year, AWS&#39;s P2 instance was produced</div>
<div id="LC717" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">using up to 16 Nvidia Tesla K80 GPUs. That month Nvidia also partnered with IBM to create a software</div>
<div id="LC718" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">kit that boosts the AI capabilities of Watson,[205] called IBM PowerAI.[206][207] Nvidia also offers its own</div>
<div id="LC719" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC720" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC721" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC722" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia Deep Learning software development kit.[208] In 2017, the GPUs were also brought online at the</div>
<div id="LC723" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Riken Center for Advanced Intelligence Project for Fujitsu.[209] The company&#39;s deep learning technology</div>
<div id="LC724" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">led to a boost in its 2017 earnings.[210]</div>
<div id="LC725" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC726" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2018, Nvidia researchers demonstrated imitation-learning techniques for industrial robots. They have</div>
<div id="LC727" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">created a system that, after a short revision and testing, can already be used to control the universal robots</div>
<div id="LC728" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of the next generation. In addition to GPU manufacturing, Nvidia provides parallel processing</div>
<div id="LC729" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">capabilities to researchers and scientists that allow them to efficiently run high-performance</div>
<div id="LC730" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">applications.[211]</div>
<div id="LC731" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC732" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Robotics</div>
<div id="LC733" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2020, Nvidia unveiled &quot;Omniverse&quot;, a virtual environment designed for engineers.[212] Nvidia also</div>
<div id="LC734" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">open-sourced Isaac Sim, which makes use of this Omniverse to train robots through simulations that</div>
<div id="LC735" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">mimic the physics of the robots and the real world.[213][214]</div>
<div id="LC736" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC737" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2024, Huang oriented Nvidia&#39;s focus towards humanoid robots and self-driving cars, which he expects</div>
<div id="LC738" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">to gain widespread adoption.[215][216]</div>
<div id="LC739" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC740" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2025, Nvidia announced Isaac GR00T N1, an open-source foundation model &quot;designed to expedite the</div>
<div id="LC741" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">development and capabilities of humanoid robots&quot;. Neura Robotics, 1X Technologies and Vention are</div>
<div id="LC742" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">among the first companies to use the model.[217][218][219]</div>
<div id="LC743" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC744" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Inception Program</div>
<div id="LC745" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia&#39;s Inception Program was created to support startups making exceptional advances in the fields of</div>
<div id="LC746" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">artificial intelligence and data science. Award winners are announced at Nvidia&#39;s GTC Conference. In</div>
<div id="LC747" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">May 2017, the program had 1,300 companies.[220] As of March 2018, there were 2,800 startups in the</div>
<div id="LC748" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Inception Program.[221] As of August 2021, the program has over 8,500 members in 90 countries, with</div>
<div id="LC749" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">cumulative funding of US$60 billion.[222]</div>
<div id="LC750" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC751" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Controversies</div>
<div id="LC752" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC753" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Maxwell advertising dispute</div>
<div id="LC754" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC755" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">GTX 970 hardware specifications</div>
<div id="LC756" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Issues with the GeForce GTX 970&#39;s specifications were first brought up by users when they found out</div>
<div id="LC757" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">that the cards, while featuring 4  GB of memory, rarely accessed memory over the 3.5  GB boundary.</div>
<div id="LC758" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Further testing and investigation eventually led to Nvidia issuing a statement that the card&#39;s initially</div>
<div id="LC759" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">announced specifications had been altered without notice before the card was made commercially</div>
<div id="LC760" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">available, and that the card took a performance hit once memory over the 3.5  GB limit were put into</div>
<div id="LC761" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">use.[223][224][225]</div>
<div id="LC762" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC763" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC764" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC765" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The card&#39;s back-end hardware specifications, initially announced as being identical to those of the</div>
<div id="LC766" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">GeForce GTX 980, differed in the amount of L2 cache (1.75 MB versus 2 MB in the GeForce GTX 980)</div>
<div id="LC767" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and the number of ROPs (56 versus 64 in the 980). Additionally, it was revealed that the card was</div>
<div id="LC768" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">designed to access its memory as a 3.5 GB section, plus a 0.5 GB one, access to the latter being 7 times</div>
<div id="LC769" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">slower than the first one.[226] The company then went on to promise a specific driver modification to</div>
<div id="LC770" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">alleviate the performance issues produced by the cutbacks suffered by the card.[227] However, Nvidia</div>
<div id="LC771" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">later clarified that the promise had been a miscommunication and there would be no specific driver</div>
<div id="LC772" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">update for the GTX 970.[228] Nvidia claimed that it would assist customers who wanted refunds in</div>
<div id="LC773" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">obtaining them.[229] On February 26, 2015, Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang went on record in Nvidia&#39;s official</div>
<div id="LC774" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">blog to apologize for the incident.[230] In February 2015 a class-action lawsuit alleging false advertising</div>
<div id="LC775" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">was filed against Nvidia and Gigabyte Technology in the U.S. District Court for Northern</div>
<div id="LC776" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">California.[231][232]</div>
<div id="LC777" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC778" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nvidia revealed that it is able to disable individual units, each containing 256  KB of L2 cache and 8</div>
<div id="LC779" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">ROPs, without disabling whole memory controllers.[233] This comes at the cost of dividing the memory</div>
<div id="LC780" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">bus into high speed and low speed segments that cannot be accessed at the same time unless one segment</div>
<div id="LC781" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">is reading while the other segment is writing because the L2/ROP unit managing both of the GDDR5</div>
<div id="LC782" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">controllers shares the read return channel and the write data bus between the two GDDR5 controllers and</div>
<div id="LC783" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">itself.[233] This is used in the GeForce GTX 970, which therefore can be described as having 3.5 GB in its</div>
<div id="LC784" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">high speed segment on a 224-bit bus and 0.5 GB in a low speed segment on a 32-bit bus.[233]</div>
<div id="LC785" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC786" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On July 27, 2016, Nvidia agreed to a preliminary settlement of the U.S. class action lawsuit,[231] offering</div>
<div id="LC787" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">a $30 refund on GTX 970 purchases. The agreed upon refund represents the portion of the cost of the</div>
<div id="LC788" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">storage and performance capabilities the consumers assumed they were obtaining when they purchased</div>
<div id="LC789" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the card.[234]</div>
<div id="LC790" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC791" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">GeForce Partner Program</div>
<div id="LC792" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The Nvidia GeForce Partner Program was a marketing program designed to provide partnering</div>
<div id="LC793" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">companies with benefits such as public relations support, video game bundling, and marketing</div>
<div id="LC794" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">development funds.[235] The program proved to be controversial, with complaints about it possibly being</div>
<div id="LC795" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">an anti-competitive practice.[236]</div>
<div id="LC796" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC797" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">First announced in a blog post on March 1, 2018,[237] it was canceled on May 4, 2018.[238]</div>
<div id="LC798" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC799" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Hardware Unboxed</div>
<div id="LC800" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On December 10, 2020, Nvidia told YouTube tech reviewer Steven Walton of Hardware Unboxed that it</div>
<div id="LC801" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">would no longer supply him with GeForce Founders Edition graphics card review units.[239][240] In a</div>
<div id="LC802" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Twitter message, Hardware Unboxed said, &quot;Nvidia have officially decided to ban us from receiving</div>
<div id="LC803" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">GeForce Founders Edition GPU review samples. Their reasoning is that we are focusing on rasterization</div>
<div id="LC804" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">instead of ray tracing. They have said they will revisit this &#39;should your editorial direction change.&#39; &quot;[241]</div>
<div id="LC805" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC806" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In emails that were disclosed by Walton from Nvidia Senior PR Manager Bryan Del Rizzo, Nvidia had</div>
<div id="LC807" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">said:</div>
<div id="LC808" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC809" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC810" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC811" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">...your GPU reviews and recommendations have continued to focus singularly on</div>
<div id="LC812" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">rasterization performance, and you have largely discounted all of the other technologies we</div>
<div id="LC813" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">offer gamers. It is very clear from your community commentary that you do not see things</div>
<div id="LC814" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the same way that we, gamers, and the rest of the industry do.[242]</div>
<div id="LC815" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC816" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">TechSpot, partner site of Hardware Unboxed, said, &quot;this and other related incidents raise serious</div>
<div id="LC817" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">questions around journalistic independence and what they are expecting of reviewers when they are sent</div>
<div id="LC818" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">products for an unbiased opinion.&quot;[242]</div>
<div id="LC819" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC820" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A number of technology reviewers came out strongly against Nvidia&#39;s move.[243][244] Linus Sebastian, of</div>
<div id="LC821" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Linus Tech Tips, titled the episode of his weekly WAN Show, &quot;NVIDIA might ACTUALLY be</div>
<div id="LC822" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">EVIL...&quot;[245] and was highly critical of the company&#39;s move to dictate specific outcomes of technology</div>
<div id="LC823" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">reviews.[246] The review site Gamers Nexus said it was, &quot;Nvidia&#39;s latest decision to shoot both its feet:</div>
<div id="LC824" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">They&#39;ve now made it so that any reviewers covering RT will become subject to scrutiny from untrusting</div>
<div id="LC825" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">viewers who will suspect subversion by the company. Shortsighted self-own from NVIDIA.&quot;[247]</div>
<div id="LC826" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC827" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Two days later, Nvidia reversed their stance.[248][249] Hardware Unboxed sent out a Twitter message, &quot;I</div>
<div id="LC828" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">just received an email from Nvidia apologizing for the previous email &amp; they&#39;ve now walked everything</div>
<div id="LC829" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">back.&quot;[250][243] On December 14, Hardware Unboxed released a video explaining the controversy from</div>
<div id="LC830" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">their viewpoint.[251] Via Twitter, they also shared a second apology sent by Nvidia&#39;s Del Rizzo that said</div>
<div id="LC831" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">&quot;to withhold samples because I didn&#39;t agree with your commentary is simply inexcusable and crossed the</div>
<div id="LC832" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">line.&quot;[252][253]</div>
<div id="LC833" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC834" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Improper disclosures about cryptomining</div>
<div id="LC835" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2018, Nvidia&#39;s chips became popular for cryptomining, the process of obtaining crypto rewards in</div>
<div id="LC836" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">exchange for verifying transactions on distributed ledgers, the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission</div>
<div id="LC837" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">(SEC) said. However, the company failed to disclose that it was a &quot;significant element&quot; of its revenue</div>
<div id="LC838" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">growth from sales of chips designed for gaming, the SEC further added in a statement and charging order.</div>
<div id="LC839" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Those omissions misled investors and analysts who were interested in understanding the impact of</div>
<div id="LC840" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">cryptomining on Nvidia&#39;s business, the SEC emphasized. Nvidia, which did not admit or deny the</div>
<div id="LC841" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">findings, has agreed to pay $5.5 million to settle civil charges, according to a statement made by the SEC</div>
<div id="LC842" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in May 2022.[254]</div>
<div id="LC843" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC844" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">French Competition Authority Investigation</div>
<div id="LC845" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On September 26, 2023, Nvidia&#39;s French offices were searched by the French Competition Authority. The</div>
<div id="LC846" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">raid, authorized by a judge, was part of an investigation into suspected anti-competitive practices in the</div>
<div id="LC847" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">graphics card sector. Nvidia has not publicly commented on the incident.[255]</div>
<div id="LC848" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC849" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">AI regulation dispute with Anthropic</div>
<div id="LC850" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In July 2025, a public dispute emerged between Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang and Anthropic CEO Dario</div>
<div id="LC851" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Amodei over AI regulation and industry practices. The conflict escalated when Amodei vehemently</div>
<div id="LC852" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">denied Huang&#39;s allegations that he sought to control the AI industry through safety concerns, calling</div>
<div id="LC853" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Huang&#39;s claims an &quot;outrageous lie.&quot;[256] The dispute centered on differing philosophies regarding AI</div>
<div id="LC854" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC855" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC856" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC857" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">development, with Amodei advocating for stronger regulatory oversight and &quot;responsible scaling</div>
<div id="LC858" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">policies,&quot; while Huang promoted open-source development and criticized what Nvidia characterized as</div>
<div id="LC859" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">&quot;regulatory capture.&quot;[256] Nvidia responded by stating that &quot;lobbying for regulatory capture against open</div>
<div id="LC860" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">source will only stifle innovation, make AI less safe and secure, and less democratic.&quot;[256] The</div>
<div id="LC861" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">controversy highlighted broader tensions within the AI industry between companies favoring rapid</div>
<div id="LC862" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">development and those emphasizing safety measures and regulation.[256]</div>
<div id="LC863" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC864" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Proposed Shanghai facility</div>
<div id="LC865" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In May 2025, U.S. senators Jim Banks and Elizabeth Warren criticized a proposed Nvidia facility in</div>
<div id="LC866" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Shanghai, saying that it &quot;raises significant national security and economic security issues that warrant</div>
<div id="LC867" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">serious review.&quot;[257]</div>
<div id="LC868" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC869" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">H20 production halt (2025)</div>
<div id="LC870" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In August 2025, Nvidia ordered suppliers to halt production of its H20 AI chip following Chinese</div>
<div id="LC871" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">government directives warning domestic companies against purchasing the processor due to security</div>
<div id="LC872" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">concerns.[258][259] The company directed suppliers including Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing</div>
<div id="LC873" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Company, Samsung Electronics, and Amkor Technology to suspend work on the China-focused</div>
<div id="LC874" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">processor.[260]</div>
<div id="LC875" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC876" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The H20 was developed in late 2023 specifically for the Chinese market to comply with U.S. export</div>
<div id="LC877" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">restrictions, featuring 96GB of HBM3 memory and 4.0 TB/s memory bandwidth—higher than the H100</div>
<div id="LC878" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">—but with significantly reduced computational power at 296 TFLOPs compared to the H100&#39;s 1979</div>
<div id="LC879" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">TFLOPs.[261][262] Despite lower raw performance, the H20 demonstrated over 20% faster performance</div>
<div id="LC880" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">than the H100 in large language model inference tasks due to architectural optimizations.[261][262]</div>
<div id="LC881" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC882" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Prior to the production halt, Nvidia had placed substantial orders for the H20, including 300,000 units</div>
<div id="LC883" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">from TSMC in July 2025, driven by strong demand from Chinese technology companies.[263] CEO</div>
<div id="LC884" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Jensen Huang denied allegations that the H20 contained security backdoors, stating the chips were</div>
<div id="LC885" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">designed solely for commercial use.[264] The production suspension occurred as Nvidia was developing</div>
<div id="LC886" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the B30A, a new chip based on its Blackwell architecture intended to succeed the H20 in the Chinese</div>
<div id="LC887" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">market.[265]</div>
<div id="LC888" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC889" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">See also</div>

<div id="LC909" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Notes</div>
<div id="LC910" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC911" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">a. Officially written as NVIDIA Corporation and stylized in its logo as nVIDIA with the</div>
<div id="LC912" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">lowercase &quot;n&quot; the same height as the uppercase &quot;VIDIA&quot;. Formerly stylized with an italicized</div>
<div id="LC913" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">lowercase &quot;n&quot;.[3] The unofficial capitalization nVidia is sometimes used by publications.[4]</div>
<div id="LC914" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
